HIGH-LEVEL DATA FLOW
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
agentview RGB 256x256
  -> native Qwen3-VL processor (correct rescale; image_grid_thw=[1,16,16])
  -> frozen 24-block Qwen3-VL vision tower -> 64 image tokens, width 2048
task text + <|fg_global|> + <|bg_global|>
  -> frozen embedding + all 28 frozen Qwen3-VL language decoder layers
XYZRGB point cloud
  -> trainable SongPointSeg -> foreground/background selection
  -> trainable foreground/background LitePT encoders -> 64-d scene features/tokens
  -> trainable FG/BG projections 64 -> 2048 inserted into the frozen prefix
noisy 32x10 action chunk + flow time
  -> action projection 10 -> 1536 + PointAction fusion
  -> 28-layer trainable Action Expert, alternating even joint-SA / odd pure-CA
  -> action output projection 1536 -> 10
Inference executes n_action_steps=16 from each predicted chunk
WorldFlow: disabled; SE(3) auxiliary branch: disabled; second camera: absent
